# Third-party notices

Arcade Clipboard is licensed under the MIT licence (see `LICENSE`). It includes the open-source parts below, each
under its own licence. This file is generated by `scripts/notices.py`; do not edit it by hand.

Scope: the Rust crates and Dart packages are those of every target (Linux, Windows, macOS, iOS and Android),
without dev dependencies; the native libraries and the packaging are those of the Linux AppImage and its
tarball. The relay (`services/relay`) is a separate server you run yourself and is not part of the app; it is
not listed. Windows, macOS, iOS and Android installers and the platform libraries they take from the operating
system or from Gradle and CocoaPods (for example Google ML Kit in the mobile builds) are not covered here.

The Flutter engine compiles the licences of its own third-party code (Skia, ICU, HarfBuzz, FreeType, ...) and of
every Dart package into `data/flutter_assets/NOTICES.Z`. That file ships in the app (in the AppImage:
`usr/lib/arcade-clipboard/data/flutter_assets/NOTICES.Z`) and **Settings > Licences** shows it.

- zerocopy-derive 0.8.59 (BSD-2-Clause OR Apache-2.0 OR MIT) — https://github.com/google/zerocopy
- zerofrom 0.1.8 (Unicode-3.0) — https://github.com/unicode-org/icu4x
- zerofrom-derive 0.1.8 (Unicode-3.0) — https://github.com/unicode-org/icu4x
- zeroize 1.9.0 (Apache-2.0 OR MIT) — https://github.com/RustCrypto/utils
- zeroize_derive 1.5.0 (Apache-2.0 OR MIT) — https://github.com/RustCrypto/utils
- zerotrie 0.2.5 (Unicode-3.0) — https://github.com/unicode-org/icu4x
- zerovec 0.11.8 (Unicode-3.0) — https://github.com/unicode-org/icu4x
- zerovec-derive 0.11.6 (Unicode-3.0) — https://github.com/unicode-org/icu4x
- zvariant 4.2.0 (MIT) — https://github.com/dbus2/zbus/
- zvariant_derive 4.2.0 (MIT) — https://github.com/dbus2/zbus/
- zvariant_utils 2.1.0 (MIT) — https://github.com/dbus2/zbus/

## C code built into the Rust core

- D-Bus 1.14.4 (libdbus, built into arcade_core by libdbus-sys; Linux only) (AFL-2.1 OR GPL-2.0-or-later) — https://www.freedesktop.org/wiki/Software/dbus/
- SQLite 3.54.1 (built into arcade_core by libsqlite3-sys) (Public domain) — https://sqlite.org/

## Vendored code

- hotkey_manager_linux 0.2.0, native part (platform/desktop/vendor/hotkey_manager_linux/linux) (MIT) — https://github.com/leanflutter/hotkey_manager

## Fonts, icons and files copied from Arcade Link

- Arcade Link v1.0.13 (a24146f): installer and shortcut assets, design kit, Arcade icons, arcade_trim.h (vendored, see scripts/VENDORED) (MIT OR Apache-2.0) — https://github.com/qa-p1/Arcade-Link
- Inter (typeface, assets/fonts/InterVariable.ttf) (OFL-1.1) — https://rsms.me/inter/
- Material Icons (fonts/MaterialIcons-Regular.otf, added by `uses-material-design`) (Apache-2.0) — https://github.com/google/material-design-icons

## Packaging

- AppImage type2-runtime (Linux AppImage) (MIT) — https://github.com/AppImage/type2-runtime
- libfuse (inside the AppImage runtime, statically linked) (LGPL-2.1-only) — https://github.com/libfuse/libfuse
- libzstd (inside the AppImage runtime) (BSD-3-Clause) — https://github.com/facebook/zstd
- musl libc (inside the AppImage runtime) (MIT) — https://musl.libc.org/
- squashfuse (inside the AppImage runtime) (BSD-2-Clause) — https://github.com/vasi/squashfuse
- zlib (inside the AppImage runtime) (Zlib) — https://zlib.net/

## Native libraries in the Linux AppImage

The Linux AppImage bundles these Ubuntu 24.04 packages' libraries next to the app (`usr/lib/arcade-clipboard/lib`).
They are dynamically linked and stored as separate files: to use a modified version of an LGPL library, run
the AppImage with `--appimage-extract`, replace the library in `squashfs-root/usr/lib/arcade-clipboard/lib`, and
start `squashfs-root/AppRun`. The source of every version of each package is published by Ubuntu at the link
given. The Flutter engine, the Rust core, the plugins and the launcher are listed above. GTK, GLib and the
other libraries the app links are not bundled; the system provides them.

- libkeybinder-3.0-0 (Ubuntu package) — https://launchpad.net/ubuntu/+source/keybinder-3.0

distributing code linked against BoringSSL does not trigger this license:
